           const size_t xm, const size_t xp,\n                                  const size_t ym, const size_t yp,\n                                  const size_t zm, const size_t zp,\n x;\n}\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,\n                        const std::vector<double>& mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * \n                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    for (const auto& val : c) { if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) { printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\"); return false; } }\n    double minVal = c[0]; double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) { minVal = std::min(minVal, val); maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val); }\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) { printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\"); return false; }\n    return true;\n}\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64, ny = 0, nz = 0; int iterations = 20; bool validate = false, printResults = false;\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) validate = true;\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) printResults = true;\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) { return 0; }\n        else { return 1; }\n    }\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx; if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    const double dx = 1.0, dy = 1.0, dz = 1.0, dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0), e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0), e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5, D = 1.0;\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize), cnew(gridSize), mu(gridSize);\n    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n        std::swap(cold, cnew);\n    }\n    if (printResults) print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    if (validate) { bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz); if (valid) { printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\"); return 0; } else { printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\"); return 1; } }\n    return 0;\n}\nEOF\ng++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -o /tmp/ch_orig/cahn_hilliard /tmp/ch_orig/cahn_hilliard.cpp Compute uses collapse(2) over (z,y) with static, effectively also contiguous chunks of rows (since collapse linearizes z*ny+y), so mapping is similar but chunk boundaries differ due to different iteration counts (vol vs ny*nz). Still pages roughly aligned.\n\nBetter to make initializeConcentration use the same collapsed (z,y) loop structure, or simply have a separate parallel first-touch loop over all three arrays using the same decomposition. g_all_cpus.clear();\n    g_primary_cpus.clear();\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<std::uint64_t>     std::snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                      \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/core_id\", cpu);\n        fp = std::fopen(path, \"r\");\n        int core = -1;\n        if (fp != double* __restrict c, const size_t idx,\n                                  const size_t xm, const size_t xp,\n                                  const size_t ym, const size_t yp,\n                                  const size_t zm, const size_t zp,\n                                  const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,\n                                  const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                  const double e_BB, const double e_AB) noexcept {\n    const double cv = c[idx];\n\n    const double cxx = (c[xp] + c[xm] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n    const * cv + cv * cv * cv\n           - gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n}\n\n// Compute the Laplacian at one grid point\ninline double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict c, const size_t idx,\n                          const size_t xm, const size_t xp,\n                          const size_t ym, const size_t yp,\n                          const size_t zm, const size_t zp,\n                          const                      const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const size_t strideZ = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = dy * laplacianAt(mu_ptr, last, last - 1, last,\n                            ym + nx - 1, yp + nx - 1,\n                            zm + nx - 1, zp + nx - 1, dx2, dy2, dz2);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict c_ptr, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t strideZ = nx * ny;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t nz_i = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(nz);\n    const std::ptrdiff_t ny_i = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(ny);\n\n    // Use the same decomposition as the compute kernels so that NUMA\n    // first-touch placement matches the threads that later use the data.\n    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (std::ptrdiff_t z = 0; z < nz_i; ++z) (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = base + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c_ptr[linear_id] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const double* __restrict c_ptr, const size_t n, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    int invalid = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for }\n    }\n    if (invalid) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c_ptr[0];\n    double maxVal = c_ptr[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c_ptr[i];\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\",   printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable   printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    omp_set_dynamic(0);\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew.get(), cold.get(), mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n\n            // Swap buffers\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(cold, cnew);\n            }\n        }\n\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external
